3. LIMITATIONS
------------------------------------------------------------------------
- This is a strict pattern matcher, not a complete JVM stack analysis.
- It intentionally rejects patterns that do not match the exact sequence.
- A match confirms only a bytecode shape, not the meaning of the field.
- Compare results with record_map.txt and paired_regions.txt.
